Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The np variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 269 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 458 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the np decomposition. By minimality, the element a^269 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 458-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
